Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the graph object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 60. The moduli space has dimension at most 70 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_60(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 60. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^70(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: Let the graph structure S satisfy the conditions of homomorphism theorem framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 3. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 947 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

For the inductive step, we decompose the graph structure using the homomorphism theorem theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 947. Each subproblem has a solution by the homomorphism theorem fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
